shrink-0 h-px w-full\"}]\n128:T77ab,"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"\u003clink rel=\"preload\" as=\"image\" href=\"/home/runner/work/dynamo/dynamo/docs-checkout/fern/assets/img/dynamo-architecture.svg\"/\u003e\u003cstyle data-precedence=\"medium\" data-href=\"fern-scroll-area-viewport\"\u003e[data-radix-scroll-area-viewport]{scrollbar-width:none;-ms-overflow-style:none;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;}[data-radix-scroll-area-viewport]::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none}\u003c/style\u003e\u003cp\u003eDynamo is a distributed inference runtime for generative AI systems that must operate at high throughput, low latency, and high reliability under changing traffic conditions. It is backend-agnostic (SGLang, TRT-LLM, vLLM, and others) and is built around three cooperating concerns:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"[\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:relative [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:absolute [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:content-[\u0026#x27;⦁\u0026#x27;] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:self-center\"\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eA fast \u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003erequest path\u003c/strong\u003e for token generation\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eA responsive \u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003econtrol path\u003c/strong\u003e for scaling and placement\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eA resilient \u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003estate path\u003c/strong\u003e for KV reuse and failure recovery\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eThis document presents Dynamo as an architecture, not a feature list: what each plane owns, how requests move, how the system adapts, and how it remains correct under failure.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch2 id=\"design-goals\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eDesign Goals\u003c/h2\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eDynamo is designed to satisfy the following goals simultaneously:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col class=\"mb-3 list-outside list-decimal [\u0026amp;_ol]:!list-[lower-roman]\"\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eLatency stability\u003c/strong\u003e: keep TTFT and ITL predictable under bursty and mixed-length traffic.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eGPU efficiency\u003c/strong\u003e: disaggregate prefill and decode so each can scale independently.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eCompute reuse\u003c/strong\u003e: minimize KV recomputation through KV-aware routing and cache lifecycle management.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eOperational resilience\u003c/strong\u003e: treat worker crashes, restarts, and overload as normal operating events.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eDeployment portability\u003c/strong\u003e: support Kubernetes-native control paths and non-Kubernetes runtime modes.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003ch2 id=\"why-this-architecture-exists\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eWhy This Architecture Exists\u003c/h2\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eModern LLM serving hits recurring bottlenecks:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"[\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:relative [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:absolute [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:content-[\u0026#x27;⦁\u0026#x27;] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:self-center\"\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003ePrefill/decode imbalance\u003c/strong\u003e leaves GPUs underutilized when traffic mix shifts (\u003ca class=\"fern-mdx-link\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer\" href=\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.09670\"\u003eDistServe\u003csvg xmlns=\"http://www.w3.org/2000/svg\" width=\"24\" height=\"24\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" class=\"lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M15 3h6v6\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M10 14 21 3\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eKV recomputation\u003c/strong\u003e increases TTFT and wastes compute when routing ignores cache overlap (\u003ca class=\"fern-mdx-link\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer\" href=\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.12948\"\u003eDeepSeek\u003csvg xmlns=\"http://www.w3.org/2000/svg\" width=\"24\" height=\"24\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" class=\"lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M15 3h6v6\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M10 14 21 3\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eMemory pressure\u003c/strong\u003e from long contexts and concurrency exceeds HBM capacity without multi-tier cache management (\u003ca class=\"fern-mdx-link\" href=\"/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/modular-components/kvbm/overview\"\u003eKVBM\u003c/a\u003e, \u003ca class=\"fern-mdx-link\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer\" href=\"https://kvcache-ai.github.io/Mooncake/design/mooncake-store.html\"\u003eMooncake\u003csvg xmlns=\"http://www.w3.org/2000/svg\" width=\"24\" height=\"24\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" class=\"lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M15 3h6v6\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M10 14 21 3\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e, \u003ca class=\"fern-mdx-link\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer\" href=\"https://blog.vllm.ai/2025/02/21/aibrix-release.html\"\u003eAIBrix\u003csvg xmlns=\"http://www.w3.org/2000/svg\" width=\"24\" height=\"24\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" class=\"lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M15 3h6v6\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M10 14 21 3\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e, \u003ca class=\"fern-mdx-link\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer\" href=\"https://github.com/taco-project/FlexKV\"\u003eFlexKV\u003csvg xmlns=\"http://www.w3.org/2000/svg\" width=\"24\" height=\"24\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" class=\"lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M15 3h6v6\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M10 14 21 3\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e, \u003ca class=\"fern-mdx-link\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer\" href=\"https://lmcache.ai/\"\u003eLMCache\u003csvg xmlns=\"http://www.w3.org/2000/svg\" width=\"24\" height=\"24\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" class=\"lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M15 3h6v6\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M10 14 21 3\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eDynamic demand\u003c/strong\u003e breaks static provisioning assumptions (\u003ca class=\"fern-mdx-link\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer\" href=\"https://github.com/Azure/AzurePublicDataset\"\u003eAzureTrace\u003csvg xmlns=\"http://www.w3.org/2000/svg\" width=\"24\" height=\"24\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" class=\"lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M15 3h6v6\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M10 14 21 3\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eReal-world failures\u003c/strong\u003e (pod restart, partition, hot-spot overload) require first-class recovery behavior.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eDynamo addresses these constraints by separating serving, control, and state propagation into explicit planes and control loops.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch2 id=\"architecture-overview\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eArchitecture Overview\u003c/h2\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan aria-owns=\"rmiz-modal-\" data-rmiz=\"\"\u003e\u003cspan data-rmiz-content=\"not-found\" style=\"visibility:visible\"\u003e\u003cimg title=\"Dynamo Architecture\" class=\"mx-auto\" src=\"/home/runner/work/dynamo/dynamo/docs-checkout/fern/assets/img/dynamo-architecture.svg\" alt=\"Dynamo architecture showing Request Plane (Client, Frontend, Router, Prefill/Decode workers), Control Plane (Planner, Dynamo Operator, Dynamo Graph, Grove, Model Express, Runtime Resources), and Storage \u0026amp; Events Plane (KVBM, NIXL, Local SSD/NFS/Remote Storage)\"/\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch2 id=\"system-model\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eSystem Model\u003c/h2\u003e\n\u003ch3 id=\"backend-execution-modes\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eBackend Execution Modes\u003c/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eDynamo supports two ways to connect an inference engine to the request plane:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"[\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:relative [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:absolute [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:content-[\u0026#x27;⦁\u0026#x27;] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:self-center\"\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eAn \u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eintegrated backend\u003c/strong\u003e runs the Dynamo worker and inference engine in the\nsame process.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eAn \u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eexperimental sidecar backend\u003c/strong\u003e runs a stock inference engine server\nbeside a CPU-only Dynamo sidecar. The request plane calls the engine’s native\ngRPC API directly, while discovery and events flow through the sidecar.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eThe sidecar mode preserves the engine’s native serve interface and separates\nengine dependencies from Dynamo, but it does not yet match the integrated\nbackends’ feature coverage. See \u003ca class=\"fern-mdx-link\" href=\"/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/concepts/system-architecture/sidecar-backends-experimental\"\u003eSidecar Backends\u003c/a\u003e for the\narchitecture and current readiness.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch3 id=\"request-plane-critical-data-path\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eRequest Plane (critical data path)\u003c/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eThe request plane is responsible for request/response execution:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"[\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:relative [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:absolute [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:content-[\u0026#x27;⦁\u0026#x27;] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:self-center\"\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eFrontend\u003c/strong\u003e accepts and normalizes requests.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eRouter\u003c/strong\u003e selects workers based on load and KV overlap.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003ePrefill workers\u003c/strong\u003e compute prompt KV state.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eDecode workers\u003c/strong\u003e generate output tokens.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eThis path is optimized for low overhead and continuous token streaming.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch3 id=\"control-plane-adaptation-and-orchestration-path\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eControl Plane (adaptation and orchestration path)\u003c/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eThe control plane is responsible for desired-state management:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"[\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:relative [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:absolute [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:content-[\u0026#x27;⦁\u0026#x27;] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:self-center\"\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003ePlanner\u003c/strong\u003e computes scaling targets from live metrics.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eDynamo Operator\u003c/strong\u003e reconciles Kubernetes resources from Dynamo CRDs.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eDiscovery + Endpoints/CRD\u003c/strong\u003e establish liveness and discoverability.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eGrove/KAI Scheduler path\u003c/strong\u003e provides topology-aware placement and grouped scaling in multinode Kubernetes deployments.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eModel Express\u003c/strong\u003e is an optional model-management endpoint when configured.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eThis path is optimized for correctness and convergence to target capacity.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch3 id=\"storage--events-plane-state-propagation-path\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eStorage \u0026amp; Events Plane (state propagation path)\u003c/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eThe storage/events plane is responsible for cache state visibility and movement:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"[\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:relative [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:absolute [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:content-[\u0026#x27;⦁\u0026#x27;] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:self-center\"\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eKV Events\u003c/strong\u003e publish cache lifecycle transitions.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eKVBM\u003c/strong\u003e manages block reuse, eviction, and offload/recall across memory tiers.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eNIXL\u003c/strong\u003e performs high-speed KV/data transfer across workers and memory domains.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eThis path is optimized for cache reuse and cross-worker handoff efficiency.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch2 id=\"end-to-end-request-narrative-disaggregated-mode\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eEnd-to-End Request Narrative (Disaggregated Mode)\u003c/h2\u003e\n\u003col class=\"mb-3 list-outside list-decimal [\u0026amp;_ol]:!list-[lower-roman]\"\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eClient sends request to \u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eFrontend\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eFrontend validates/preprocesses and forwards to \u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eRouter\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eRouter chooses a \u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003ePrefill worker\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003ePrefill computes KV and returns transfer metadata.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eRouter chooses a \u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eDecode worker\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eDecode receives KV state (typically via \u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eNIXL\u003c/strong\u003e transfer path).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eDecode streams tokens back through Frontend.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eKV Events\u003c/strong\u003e update cache visibility for future routing decisions.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eKVBM\u003c/strong\u003e may offload or recall KV blocks based on pressure and reuse potential.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eFor flow-level detail, see \u003ca class=\"fern-mdx-link\" href=\"/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/concepts/system-architecture/architecture-flow\"\u003eArchitecture Flow\u003c/a\u003e.\nFor request transport options, see \u003ca class=\"fern-mdx-link\" href=\"/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/concepts/communication-planes/request-plane\"\u003eRequest Plane\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch2 id=\"control-loops\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eControl Loops\u003c/h2\u003e\n\u003ch3 id=\"serving-loop\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eServing Loop\u003c/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eMaintains low-latency request execution across frontend, router, prefill, and decode workers.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch3 id=\"planning-loop\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003ePlanning Loop\u003c/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eMaintains capacity alignment with demand:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"[\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:relative [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:absolute [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:content-[\u0026#x27;⦁\u0026#x27;] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:self-center\"\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003ePlanner consumes runtime metrics.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003ePlanner computes prefill/decode targets.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eConnector layer applies targets to runtime resources.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp\u003ePlanner supports throughput-based and load-based strategies. See \u003ca class=\"fern-mdx-link\" href=\"/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/modular-components/planner/planner-design\"\u003ePlanner Design\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch3 id=\"resilience-loop\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eResilience Loop\u003c/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eMaintains system continuity under failure:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"[\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:relative [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:absolute [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:content-[\u0026#x27;⦁\u0026#x27;] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:self-center\"\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eHealth checks detect unhealthy workers.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eDiscovery liveness removes stale endpoints.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eGraceful shutdown drains in-flight work.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eRequest migration/cancellation controls in-flight behavior.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eLoad shedding prevents cascading collapse under overload.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eSee \u003ca class=\"fern-mdx-link\" href=\"/dynamo/dev/kubernetes/fault-tolerance/overview\"\u003eFault Tolerance\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch2 id=\"kubernetes-native-realization-crd--grove\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eKubernetes-Native Realization (CRD + Grove)\u003c/h2\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eIn Kubernetes deployments, the same architecture maps to declarative resources:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"[\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:relative [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:absolute [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:content-[\u0026#x27;⦁\u0026#x27;] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:self-center\"\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eDynamo Operator reconciles \u003ccode\u003eDynamoGraphDeployment\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eDiscoverability is derived from \u003ccode\u003eDynamoWorkerMetadata\u003c/code\u003e + EndpointSlices.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eGrove-backed multinode deployments model worker groups as \u003ccode\u003ePodCliqueSet\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003ePodClique\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003eIndependent prefill/decode elasticity is represented via \u003ccode\u003ePodCliqueScalingGroup\u003c/code\u003e with separate \u003ccode\u003ereplicas\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003emin\u003c/code\u003e targets.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eThe diagram labels such as \u003ccode\u003ePodClique A/B\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eScalingGroup \u0026quot;Prefill\u0026quot;\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eScalingGroup \u0026quot;Decode\u0026quot;\u003c/code\u003e, and \u003ccode\u003e(replicas, min)\u003c/code\u003e represent this grouped scaling model.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch2 id=\"request-routing-topologies\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eRequest Routing Topologies\u003c/h2\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eThe request plane can be exposed in two ways:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"[\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:relative [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:text-(color:--grayscale-a10) mb-3 list-none pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:pl-3 [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:absolute [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:ml-[-22px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:mt-[-1px] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:content-[\u0026#x27;⦁\u0026#x27;] [\u0026amp;\u0026gt;li]:before:self-center\"\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eDynamo-native Frontend routing\u003c/strong\u003e — the Dynamo Frontend is the request entry point and the integrated Dynamo Router selects workers using KV-aware scoring.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"\"\u003e\u003cstrong class=\"font-semibold\"\u003eGateway API routing with GAIE\u003c/strong\u003e — a Kubernetes \u003ca class=\"fern-mdx-link\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noreferrer\" href=\"https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/gateway-api-inference-extension\"\u003eGateway API Inference Extension\u003csvg xmlns=\"http://www.w3.org/2000/svg\" width=\"24\" height=\"24\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" fill=\"none\" stroke=\"currentColor\" stroke-width=\"2\" stroke-linecap=\"round\" stroke-linejoin=\"round\" class=\"lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M15 3h6v6\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M10 14 21 3\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003cpath d=\"M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e gateway calls the Dynamo Endpoint Picker Plugin (EPP), then forwards to the selected worker’s Frontend sidecar in \u003ccode\u003e--router-mode direct\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eBoth topologies share the same control plane, event plane, and backend integrations; only the request entry point and routing boundary differ. See \u003ca class=\"fern-mdx-link\" href=\"/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/kubernetes/kubernetes-operator/gateway-api-routing\"\u003eGateway API Routing Architecture\u003c/a\u003e for the component and request-flow details.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch2 id=\"fault-tolerance-architecture\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eFault Tolerance Architecture\u003c/h2\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eFault tolerance is embedded across layers:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"fern-table-root not-prose\" data-state=\"closed\"\u003e\u003cdiv dir=\"ltr\" class=\"fern-scroll-area\" style=\"position:relative;--radix-scroll-area-corner-width:0px;--radix-scroll-area-corner-height:0px\"\u003e\u003cdiv data-radix-scroll-area-viewport=\"\" class=\"fern-scroll-area-viewport\" data-scrollbars=\"both\" style=\"overflow-x:hidden;overflow-y:hidden\"\u003e\u003cdiv style=\"min-width:100%;display:table\"\u003e\u003ctable class=\"fern-table\"\u003e\u003cthead\u003e\u003ctr\u003e\u003cth\u003eLayer\u003c/th\u003e\u003cth\u003eMechanism\u003c/th\u003e\u003cth\u003ePractical effect\u003c/th\u003e\u003c/tr\u003e\u003c/thead\u003e\u003ctbody\u003e\u003ctr\u003e\u003ctd\u003eRequest\u003c/td\u003e\u003ctd\u003eMigration, cancellation\u003c/td\u003e\u003ctd\u003eIn-flight work can continue or terminate intentionally\u003c/td\u003e\u003c/tr\u003e\u003ctr\u003e\u003ctd\u003eWorker\u003c/td\u003e\u003ctd\u003eHealth checks, graceful shutdown, endpoint draining\u003c/td\u003e\u003ctd\u003eFailed/terminating workers stop taking new traffic safely\u003c/td\u003e\u003c/tr\u003e\u003ctr\u003e\u003ctd\u003eSystem\u003c/td\u003e\u003ctd\u003eRequest rejection/load shedding\u003c/td\u003e\u003ctd\u003ePrevents overload from propagating across workers\u003c/td\u003e\u003c/tr\u003e\u003ctr\u003e\u003ctd\u003eInfrastructure\u003c/td\u003e\u003ctd\u003eDiscovery lease expiry, event-path recovery\u003c/td\u003e\u003ctd\u003eStale membership is removed and traffic reroutes\u003c/td\u003e\u003c/tr\u003e\u003c/tbody\u003e\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eThis model assumes failures are routine, not exceptional.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch2 id=\"performance-rationale\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003ePerformance Rationale\u003c/h2\u003e\n\u003ch3 id=\"disaggregated-serving\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eDisaggregated Serving\u003c/h3\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eSeparating prefill and decode improves utilization and enables phase-specific scaling.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan aria-owns=\"rmiz-modal-\" data-rmiz=\"\"\u003e\u003cspan data-rmiz-content=\"not-found\" style=\"visibility:visible\"\u003e\u003cimg on H100 with R1 Distilled Llama 70B FP8 on vLLM. 3K ISL / 150 OSL.\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003ch3 id=\"kv-aware-routing\" data-state=\"closed\" style=\"cursor:pointer\"\u003eKV-Aware It is backend-agnostic (SGLang, TRT-LLM, vLLM, and others) and is built around three cooperating concerns:\n\n- A fast **request path** for token generation\n- A responsive **control path** for scaling and placement\n- A resilient **state path** for KV reuse and failure recovery\n\nThis document presents Dynamo as an architecture, not a feature list: what each plane owns, how requests move, how the system adapts, and how it remains correct under failure.\n\n## Design Goals\n\nDynamo is designed to satisfy the following goals simultaneously:\n\n1. **Latency stability**: keep TTFT and ITL predictable under bursty and mixed-length traffic.\n2. **GPU efficiency**: disaggregate prefill and decode so each can scale independently.\n3. **Compute reuse**: minimize KV recomputation through KV-aware routing and cache lifecycle management.\n4. **Operational resilience**: treat worker crashes, restarts, and overload as normal operating events.\n5. **Deployment portability**: support Kubernetes-native control paths and non-Kubernetes runtime modes.\n\n## Why This Architecture Exists\n\nModern LLM serving hits recurring bottlenecks:\n\n- **Prefill/decode imbalance** leaves GPUs underutilized when traffic mix shifts ([DistServe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.09670)).\n- **KV recomputation** increases TTFT and wastes compute when routing ignores cache overlap ([DeepSeek](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.12948)).\n- **Memory pressure** from long contexts and concurrency exceeds HBM capacity without multi-tier cache management ([KVBM](/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/modular-components/kvbm/overview), [Mooncake](https://kvcache-ai.github.io/Mooncake/design/mooncake-store.html), [AIBrix](https://blog.vllm.ai/2025/02/21/aibrix-release.html), [FlexKV](https://github.com/taco-project/FlexKV), [LMCache](https://lmcache.ai/)).\n- **Dynamic demand** breaks static provisioning assumptions ([AzureTrace](https://github.com/Azure/AzurePublicDataset)).\n- **Real-world failures** (pod restart, partition, hot-spot overload) require first-class recovery behavior.\n\nDynamo addresses these constraints by separating serving, control, and state propagation into explicit planes and control loops.\n\n## Architecture Overview\n\n![Dynamo architecture showing Request Plane (Client, Frontend, Router, Prefill/Decode workers), Control Plane (Planner, Dynamo Operator, Dynamo Graph, Grove, Model Express, Runtime Resources), and Storage \u0026amp; Events Plane (KVBM, NIXL, Local SSD/NFS/Remote Storage)](/home/runner/work/dynamo/dynamo/docs-checkout/fern/assets/img/dynamo-architecture.svg \"Dynamo Architecture\")\n\n## System Model\n\n### Backend Execution Modes\n\nDynamo supports two ways to connect an inference engine to the request plane:\n\n- An **integrated backend** runs the Dynamo worker and inference engine in the\n  same process.\n- An **experimental sidecar backend** runs a stock inference engine server\n  beside a CPU-only Dynamo sidecar. The request plane calls the engine's native\n  gRPC API directly, while discovery and events flow through the sidecar.\n\nThe sidecar mode preserves the engine's native serve interface and separates\nengine dependencies from Dynamo, but it does not yet match the integrated\nbackends' feature coverage. See [Sidecar Backends](/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/concepts/system-architecture/sidecar-backends-experimental) for the\narchitecture and current readiness.\n\n### Request Plane (critical data path)\n\nThe request plane is responsible for request/response execution:\n\n- **Frontend** accepts and normalizes requests.\n- **Router** selects workers based on load and KV overlap.\n- **Prefill workers** compute prompt KV state.\n- **Decode workers** generate output tokens.\n\nThis path is optimized for low overhead and continuous token streaming.\n\n### Control Plane (adaptation and orchestration path)\n\nThe control plane is responsible for desired-state management:\n\n- **Planner** computes scaling targets from live metrics.\n- **Dynamo Operator** reconciles Kubernetes resources from Dynamo CRDs.\n- **Discovery + Endpoints/CRD** establish liveness and discoverability.\n- **Grove/KAI Scheduler path** provides topology-aware placement and grouped scaling in multinode Kubernetes deployments.\n- **Model Express** is an optional model-management endpoint when configured.\n\nThis path is optimized for correctness and convergence to target capacity.\n\n### Storage \u0026 Events Plane (state propagation path)\n\nThe storage/events plane is responsible for cache state visibility and movement:\n\n- **KV Events** publish cache lifecycle transitions.\n- **KVBM** manages block reuse, eviction, and offload/recall across memory tiers.\n- **NIXL** performs high-speed KV/data transfer across workers and memory domains.\n\nThis path is optimized for cache reuse and cross-worker handoff efficiency.\n\n## End-to-End Request Narrative (Disaggregated Mode)\n\n1. Client sends request to **Frontend**.\n2. Frontend validates/preprocesses and forwards to **Router**.\n3. Router chooses a **Prefill worker**.\n4. Prefill computes KV and returns transfer metadata.\n5. Router chooses a **Decode worker**.\n6. Decode receives KV state (typically via **NIXL** transfer path).\n7. Decode streams tokens back through Frontend.\n8. **KV Events** update cache visibility for future routing decisions.\n9. **KVBM** may offload or recall KV blocks based on pressure and reuse potential.\n\nFor flow-level detail, see [Architecture Flow](/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/concepts/system-architecture/architecture-flow).\nFor request transport options, see [Request Plane](/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/concepts/communication-planes/request-plane).\n\n## Control Loops\n\n### Serving Loop\n\nMaintains low-latency request execution across frontend, router, prefill, and decode workers.\n\n### Planning Loop\n\nMaintains capacity alignment with demand:\n\n- Planner consumes runtime metrics.\n- Planner computes prefill/decode targets.\n- Connector layer applies targets to runtime resources.\n\nPlanner supports throughput-based and load-based strategies. See [Planner Design](/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/modular-components/planner/planner-design).\n\n### Resilience Loop\n\nMaintains system continuity under failure:\n\n- Health checks detect unhealthy workers.\n- Discovery liveness removes stale endpoints.\n- Graceful shutdown drains in-flight work.\n- Request migration/cancellation controls in-flight behavior.\n- Load shedding prevents cascading collapse under overload.\n\nSee [Fault Tolerance](/dynamo/dev/kubernetes/fault-tolerance/overview).\n\n## Kubernetes-Native Realization (CRD + Grove)\n\nIn Kubernetes deployments, the same architecture maps to declarative resources:\n\n- Dynamo Operator reconciles `DynamoGraphDeployment`.\n- Discoverability is derived from `DynamoWorkerMetadata` + EndpointSlices.\n- Grove-backed multinode deployments model worker groups as `PodCliqueSet` and `PodClique`.\n- Independent prefill/decode elasticity is represented via `PodCliqueScalingGroup` with separate `replicas` and `min` targets.\n\nThe diagram labels such as `PodClique A/B`, `ScalingGroup \"Prefill\"`, `ScalingGroup \"Decode\"`, and `(replicas, min)` represent this grouped scaling model.\n\n## Request Routing Topologies\n\nThe request plane can be exposed in two ways:\n\n- **Dynamo-native Frontend routing** -- the Dynamo Frontend is the request entry point and the integrated Dynamo Router selects workers using KV-aware scoring.\n- **Gateway API routing with GAIE** -- a Kubernetes [Gateway API Inference Extension](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/gateway-api-inference-extension) gateway calls the Dynamo Endpoint Picker Plugin (EPP), then forwards to the selected worker's Frontend sidecar in `--router-mode direct`.\n\nBoth topologies share the same control plane, event plane, and backend integrations; only the request entry point and routing boundary differ. See [Gateway API Routing Architecture](/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/kubernetes/kubernetes-operator/gateway-api-routing) for the component and request-flow details.\n\n## Fault Tolerance Architecture\n\nFault tolerance is embedded across layers:\n\n| Layer | Mechanism | Practical effect |\n|------|-----------|------------------|\n| Request | Migration, cancellation | In-flight work can continue or terminate intentionally |\n| Worker | Health checks, graceful shutdown, endpoint draining | Failed/terminating workers stop taking new traffic safely |\n| System | Request rejection/load shedding | Prevents overload from propagating across workers |\n| Infrastructure | Discovery lease expiry, event-path recovery | Stale membership is removed and traffic reroutes |\n\nThis model assumes failures are routine, not exceptional.\n\n## Performance Rationale\n\n### Disaggregated Serving\n\nSeparating prefill and decode improves utilization and enables phase-specific scaling.\n\n![Two scatter plots comparing the performance of disagg and baseline configurations on one node versus two nodes](file:assets/img/disagg-perf-benefit.png)\n\n*Tested on H100 with R1 Distilled Llama 70B FP8 on vLLM. 3K ISL / 150 OSL.*\n\n### KV-Aware Routing\n\nRouting with cache overlap + load signals reduces prefill recomputation and improves latency.\nFor an external production case study, see [How Baseten achieved 2x faster inference with NVIDIA Dynamo](https://www.baseten.co/blog/how-baseten-achieved-2x-faster-inference-with-nvidia-dynamo/#how-baseten-uses-nvidia-dynamo).\n\n![Two bar charts comparing Random routing and Dynamo with KV aware routing for Time To First Token (3x faster with Dynamo) and Avg request latency (2x faster with Dynamo).](file:assets/img/kv-routing.png)\n\n*Tested with 100K requests to R1 using R1 Distilled Llama 70B FP8 on 2 H100 nodes. Avg 4K ISL / 800 OSL.*\n\n### KV Block Manager (KVBM)\n\nKVBM extends effective cache capacity using multi-tier memory offload/recall.\n\n![Line graph comparing Pure GPU prefix caching with vLLM and KVBM host offloading for TTFT (Time To First Token)](file:assets/img/kvbm-agg-performance.png)\n\n*Tested across QPS values using Qwen3-8B on H100. Avg 20K ISL / 100 OSL.*\n\n### NIXL Data Transfer\n\nNIXL reduces KV handoff cost in distributed serving by optimizing cross-worker transfer behavior across heterogeneous memory.\n\n## Implementation Model\n\n- **Rust** for performance-sensitive runtime components.\n- **Python** for backend integration and extensibility.\n- Modular subsystem boundaries so routing, planning, memory, and transport can evolve independently.\n\n## Related Documentation\n\n- [Architecture Flow](/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/concepts/system-architecture/architecture-flow)\n- [Router Design](/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/modular-components/router/router-design)\n- [Planner Design](/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/modular-components/planner/planner-design)\n- [Discovery Plane](/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/concepts/communication-planes/discovery-plane)\n- [Event Plane](/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/concepts/communication-planes/event-plane)\n- [Request Plane](/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/concepts/communication-planes/request-plane)\n- [Fault Tolerance](/dynamo/dev/kubernetes/fault-tolerance/overview)\n- [Grove](/dynamo/dev/knowledge-base/kubernetes/multinode/grove)\n\n## Acknowledgements\n\nDynamo is informed by prior open-source work from:\n\n- vLLM\n- SGLang\n- DistServe\n- Mooncake\n- AIBrix\n-
